Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [216] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)的新蒸馏方法，通过引入delta信号（教师模型与其基础模型在推理指令微调前的差异）替代直接模仿教师输出分布，在数学、科学和代码推理基准上显著优于传统on-policy蒸馏，并能以短时后训练实现强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的on-policy蒸馏直接模仿教师模型的输出分布，但这种方式未充分利用教师模型在推理指令微调过程中产生的变化，限制了推理能力的有效迁移。作者希望提供更直接的推理能力迁移信号。

Method: 定义delta信号为教师模型与其基础模型（推理指令微调前）的输出差异，该信号捕捉了推理微调带来的变化。基于此设计新的蒸馏奖励，替代直接模仿教师输出分布，形成On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)方法。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理基准上的实验表明，OPD²始终优于传统on-policy蒸馏方法，且仅需短时后训练即可使推理LLM达到强性能。

Conclusion: delta信号为推理能力迁移提供了更直接有效的监督信号，OPD²是一种高效且性能优越的on-policy蒸馏改进方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

</details>


### [217] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合神经-物理框架，将已知ODE分量保持显式、未知分量用神经网络表示，通过RTS平滑器和反向传播交替迭代，实现从不完整测量数据中学习缺失的动力学方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际动力学系统中部分方程未知且仅能观测部分状态变量，现有方法难以同时利用已知物理结构和学习缺失动力学。

Method: 两阶段交替迭代：第一阶段将模型参数视为已知，用RTS平滑器从测量数据推断潜在状态；第二阶段将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力学基准系统上，该方法能从部分观测中学习缺失ODE分量，改善潜在状态重构和长时域预测。

Conclusion: 混合神经-物理框架能利用已知机理结构学习缺失动力学，保持模型可解释性，适用于部分状态观测场景。

Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [218] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BadWAM框架，系统研究了世界-行动模型（WAM）面临的对抗性攻击。通过引入两类攻击——纯动作攻击（直接导致任务失败）和保持想象攻击（不改变未来预测但导致动作偏移），揭示了WAM中"想象与执行"之间对齐的脆弱性。实验表明这些攻击能显著降低任务成功率（如从96.5%降至43.1%），且保持未来预测一致性的攻击仍能高效破坏动作执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM研究假设其表征空间中的动作生成与未来世界预测之间的耦合能带来鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性。本文质疑这一假设，旨在揭示这类耦合本身存在可被利用的脆弱性，即攻击者可以通过微小视觉扰动打破"想象"与"执行"之间的一致性。

Method: 提出BadWAM统一框架，定义"世界-行动漂移攻击"（World-Action Drift Attacks）新攻击类别。沿两个维度（攻击强度与隐蔽性）设计两类攻击：1) 纯动作对抗攻击（action-only），直接驱动模型产生导致任务失败的动作；2) 保持想象对抗攻击（imagination-preserving），在保持未来预测接近干净样本的前提下诱导有害动作偏移。攻击在闭环执行设置下进行评估。

Result: 攻击显著降低WAM在闭环任务中的成功率：纯动作攻击将性能从96.5%降至43.1%；保持想象攻击也表现出强攻击性能，同时未来想象漂移较小，揭示了WAM特有的安全漏洞。

Conclusion: WAM中想象与执行的对齐假设是脆弱的，微小视觉扰动即可解耦两者。BadWAM为评估WAM鲁棒性提供了统一框架，并强调了这一新兴范式面临的特有安全挑战。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

</details>


### [219] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对未知寿命分布下独立同构机器的块替换策略，提出基于多臂老虎机的数据驱动算法来学习最优替换区间k*，实现O(K log T)的累积遗憾，并利用块替换的嵌套观测特性进一步降低遗憾至O((K-k*) log T)。同时提出Kaplan-Meier更新算法实现非参数分布估计与策略一致性，并分析两种平均成本MDP模型验证块替换的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在块替换策略中，操作员需从混合了完全失效和右删失的寿命数据中学习未知的寿命分布，以最小化单位时间成本。现有方法通常假设分布已知，本文旨在开发数据驱动算法，在无先验分布知识的情况下自适应地学习最优替换区间。

Method: 将问题建模为随机多臂老虎机，提出基于Hoeffding和Bernstein不等式的置信下界算法；利用块替换中嵌套观测（较短区间包含较长区间的失效数据）特性，设计相关变体算法；提出Kaplan-Meier更新算法非参数估计寿命分布；建立两种平均成本MDP模型（时间流逝模型和年龄向量模型）分析最优策略结构。

Result: Hoeffding/Bernstein算法实现O(K log T)遗憾，匹配Lai-Robbins下界；相关变体算法实现O((K-k*)log T)遗憾，且对k<k*的次优臂仅需O(1)次直接拉动；Kaplan-Meier更新算法几乎必然收敛到最优策略，长时域下增量遗憾趋近于零；数值实验验证了理论排序并揭示了块替换与年龄依赖替换之间的结构性成本差距。

Conclusion: 本文提出的数据驱动算法能够在未知寿命分布下高效学习块替换策略的最优区间，理论遗憾界紧致且经验性能优异。MDP分析为块替换策略提供了最优性保证，并建立了与年龄依赖替换策略的性能基准对比。

Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

</details>


### [220] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可变草图（Mutable Sketches）方法，通过KP-tree存储用户偏好、一次性拟合低秩投影，实现流式实时更新嵌入向量，无需模型重训练。在KuaiRec数据集上以1.8%数据读取量达到0.810 RMSE（ALS为0.822），新用户首次评分后<1ms即可获得个性化推荐，且证明了预测误差包络单调收紧的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两阶段推荐系统中嵌入向量陈旧性（embedding staleness）问题——用户对新物品评分后，其嵌入向量需等到下一个重训练周期才能更新，导致推荐实时性差。

Method: 提出可变草图，核心组件为KP-tree（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）存储用户偏好，一次拟合低秩投影，评分到达时在线重计算嵌入向量。采用KP-tree的范数比例采样策略提升稀疏数据上的物品覆盖率。

Result: KuaiRec上：可变草图RMSE 0.810（仅读取1.8%数据），ALS为0.822（100%数据）；每批更新速度提升8倍；新用户首次评分后<1ms获得个性化推荐；稀疏数据（<1%密度）上物品覆盖率提升40-130%。

Conclusion: 可变草图方法在推荐实时性、数据效率和更新速度上显著优于传统两阶段方法，且有严格的收敛保证。KP-tree的范数比例采样在稀疏场景下优势明显，密集场景下均匀采样即可。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>


### [221] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体推文的新方法，用于分析比特币市场情绪，而非预测价格。通过多模型对比，XGBoost在情绪分类任务中表现最佳（F1≈0.84），并利用SHAP增强模型可解释性，揭示了链上特征对情绪预测的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币作为去中心化数字资产和投资工具的广泛应用，引发了对其市场行为理解的强烈需求。现有研究多聚焦于价格预测，而本研究旨在解释市场情绪，填补从多源数据理解比特币市场情绪的空白。

Method: 融合区块链交易数据、比特币历史价格数据和每日Twitter情绪分类结果，将情绪趋势与链上和金融指标归一化处理后构建数据集；采用多种机器学习模型进行交叉验证，并以XGBoost作为最优模型；使用SHAP方法量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献，提升模型可解释性。

Result: XGBoost在情绪分类任务中取得了约0.84的平均F1分数，优于其他模型；SHAP分析成功量化了链上特征的重要性，表明所采用的数据组合能够产生有意义的预测信号。

Conclusion: 结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体数据能够有效解释比特币市场情绪，支持数据驱动的加密货币分析，并为未来引入深度学习改进提供了方向。

Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [The Cost and Network Limits of Space-Based AI Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14172)
*Kees van Berkel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了低地球轨道（LEO）大规模AI数据中心作为地面设施成本效益替代方案的可行性，通过比较发射成本、发电、冷却、辐射暴露、大气再入以及计算网络性能，发现LEO推理可能可行，但训练前沿规模LLM在轨道上难以与地面数据中心竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将大规模AI数据中心部署在低地球轨道是否能在成本效益上替代传统地面数据中心，以应对地面数据中心的能耗、冷却等限制。

Method: 对比分析轨道与地面系统在发射成本、发电、冷却、辐射暴露、大气再入等方面的差异，并引入从地面Clos网络到空间激光星间链路网状网络的转变，利用对分带宽、对分强度和类屋顶线模型进行评估。

Result: 基于LEO的推理可能可行，但训练前沿规模的大语言模型在轨道上难以与地面数据中心竞争。

Conclusion: 低地球轨道AI数据中心在推理任务上具有一定潜力，但在训练大规模模型方面不具备与地面设施竞争的成本效益。

Abstract: This paper evaluates whether large-scale AI data centers deployed in low-Earth orbit (LEO) could become a cost-effective alternative to terrestrial facilities. The analysis compares orbital and ground-based systems across launch cost, power generation, cooling, radiation exposure, and atmospheric reentry, as well as compute-network performance. A key distinction is the shift from terrestrial Clos networks to space-based mesh networks using laser inter-satellite links. Using bisection bandwidth, bisection intensity, and roofline-style models, we show that while LEO-based inference may be feasible, training frontier-scale LLMs in orbit is unlikely to be competitive with terrestrial data centers.

</details>


### [223] [DRIFT: Direct Reduced Fourier Transforms for Distributed Spectral Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14394)
*Sana Taghipour Anvari,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式截断谱变换（DTST）和其GPU实现DRIFT，通过先局部计算再通信的方式优化傅里叶神经算子（FNO）在高分辨率网格上的分布式计算，相比基线在3D+time FNO上实现38-64倍前向加速和37倍训练加速，通信占比从97%降至6%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO在高分辨率空间网格上需要跨GPU分布数据，但标准分布式FFT中每个光谱层需要密集的全连接通信（all-to-all）传输完整空间张量，而大部分系数随即被截断丢弃，造成极大的通信开销（占前向时间97%），严重限制了FNO的可扩展性。

Method: 提出DTST（Distributed Truncated Spectral Transform），反转传统计算顺序：每个GPU先通过部分DFT本地计算少量频率模式，再通过两次集合通信合并结果，通信负载仅取决于保留的模式数量而非空间分辨率。基于DTST实现DRIFT系统，采用可分离的逐维基矩阵和高效的GPU间通信。

Result: 在4-32个GPU（最多8节点，每节点4GPU）的3D+time FNO上，前向计算加速38-64倍，训练加速37倍，通信时间占比从97%降至6%以下，且在高分辨率下加速比更大。

Conclusion: DTST/DRIFT通过重新设计计算-通信顺序，有效解决了分布式FNO中FFT通信瓶颈问题，同时保持数值精度，实现了接近线性的可扩展性，使FNO能够高效处理更高分辨率的计算物理问题。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) learn solution operators for partial differential equations and offer orders of magnitude speedup over traditional numerical solvers at inference time, which makes them attractive surrogates for high-resolution computational physics. Scaling FNOs to high-resolution spatial grids requires distributing the data across GPUs, but the distributed FFT at the core of each spectral layer requires multiple dense all-to-all collectives that communicate the full spatial tensor, only for most coefficients to be discarded immediately. We introduce the Distributed Truncated Spectral Transform (DTST), which reverses this order. Each GPU computes only a small subset of frequency modes used by the spectral convolution locally via a partial DFT, and two collectives combine the results with a payload that depends only on this mode count, not the spatial resolution. DTST produces spectral coefficients identical to the standard distributed FFT with truncation, while providing both spatial data parallelism and spectral weight model parallelism. We present DRIFT, a GPU implementation of DTST for distributed Fourier Neural Operators, using separable per-dimension basis matrices and efficient GPU-to-GPU communication. On a 3D+time FNO across 4--32 GPUs, on up to 8 nodes (4 GPUs/node), DRIFT achieves a forward-pass speedup of 38--64$\times$ and a 37$\times$ training speedup over the distributed FNO baseline, reducing communication time from 97\% to under 6\% of the forward-pass time, with growing speedups at higher resolution.

</details>


### [224] [Settling The Round Complexity of Byzantine Agreement Against a Full-Information, Adaptive Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14413)
*Yuval Efron*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明在全信息强自适应敌手模型下，容忍t个腐败方的随机同步拜占庭协议的最坏情况期望轮复杂度为Ω(t²/(n log(n+1)))，改进了Bar-Joseph和Ben-Or (1998)的经典下界Ω(t/√(n log n))，并在t<<n regime下与Dufoulon和Pandurangan (2025)的上界O(min{t² log n/n, t/log n})仅差log² n因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进随机同步拜占庭协议轮复杂度的下界，缩小与上界之间的差距。

Method: 1) 证明一个多轮集中引理：任何概率为p的转录事件可以通过期望腐败O(√(n log(1/p)))个节点被强制发生；2) 利用Chor、Merritt和Shmoys (1989)的工具，通过一个涉及至多R个节点的崩溃调度，将协议在R轮内未结束的概率下界为1/n^{O(R)}。

Result: 最坏情况期望轮复杂度下界为Ω(t²/(n log(n+1)))，改进了经典下界Ω(t/√(n log n))。

Conclusion: 该下界与现有上界在t<<n时仅差log² n因子，大大缩小了随机拜占庭协议轮复杂度的理论界限差距。

Abstract: We prove that every randomized synchronous Byzantine Agreement protocol in the full-information, strongly adaptive adversary model, secure against $t$ corrupt parties, has worst-case expected round complexity \[
  Ω\!\left(\frac{t^2}{n\log(n+1)}\right). \] This improves upon the seminal $Ω(\frac{t}{\sqrt{n\log n}})$ bound of [Bar-Joseph, Ben-Or 98]. Our result matches the recent upper bound of $O\left(\min\left\{\frac{t^2\log n}{n},\frac{t}{\log n}\right\}\right)$ of [Dufoulon, Pandurangan 25], up to a $\log^2 n$ factor in the $t\ll n$ regime. Our proof takes inspiration from the recent works of [Etesami, Mahloujifar, Mahmoody 20] and [Haitner, Karidi-Heller 26]. Specifically, we prove a multi-round concentration lemma showing that any transcript event of probability $p$ can be forced with probability one by corrupting $O(\sqrt{n\log(\frac1p)})$ parties in expectation. From there, tools from [Chor, Merritt, Shmoys 89] allow us to lower-bound the probability of the protocol not concluding in $R$ rounds by $\frac{1}{n^{O(R)}}$, using a crash schedule involving at most $R$ parties. The combination of these techniques yields the desired bound.

</details>


### [225] [EdgeFaaS: A Function-based Framework for Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14489)
*Neha Vadnere,Yu-Ting Wang,Yitao Chen,Sreehari Sadesh,Ming Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出EdgeFaaS，一个基于函数的边缘计算框架，通过函数虚拟化和存储虚拟化抽象分布式异构资源，支持多样化的边缘计算工作流，并在100+真实地理分布式设备上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算资源在能力和容量上高度异构且分布广泛，现有分布式计算框架无法充分处理这种异构性和分布性。

Method: 提出函数虚拟化和存储虚拟化来抽象分布式异构物理资源，提供一致的虚拟接口用于函数部署执行和数据存储访问；支持多样化的边缘计算工作流，允许用户灵活调整配置并探索重要权衡。

Result: 在100+地理分布的IoT设备、边缘服务器和云服务上实现了三种代表性工作流（视频分析、联邦学习、音频分类），验证了框架的可用性。

Conclusion: EdgeFaaS能有效使边缘应用利用分布于IoT、边缘和云端的异构资源，用户可灵活探索部署配置与性能权衡（如计算-通信成本权衡、训练精度-速度权衡）。

Abstract: Edge computing brings unique challenges as the resources on the edge are highly diverse in capabilities and capacities, and highly distributed across many users and the physical world. Existing distributed computing frameworks cannot adequately handle this level of heterogeneity and distribution. This paper proposes EdgeFaaS, a novel function-based edge computing framework to enable edge applications to effectively utilize heterogeneous resources distributed across the Internet of Things (IoT), edge, and cloud for computing. It proposes function virtualization and storage virtualization to abstract distributed and heterogeneous physical resources and provides consistent virtual interfaces for deploying and executing functions and storing and accessing data. EdgeFaaS provides comprehensive support to diverse edge computing workflows, and at the same time allows users to flexibly adjust the configurations and explore various important tradeoffs. To demonstrate its usability, the paper also presents the implementation and evaluation of three representative workflows on EdgeFaaS for video analytics, federated learning, and audio classification, on a real testbed of 100+ geographically distributed IoT devices, edge servers, and cloud services. EdgeFaaS allows users to flexibly explore the deployment configurations of these workflows over distributed and heterogeneous resources. For example, users can easily vary the function placement of the video processing pipeline across IoT, edge, and cloud resources and study the tradeoff between computation and communication costs; users can also flexibly adjust the cluster count and size in the hierarchical federated learning system and explore the tradeoff between training accuracy and speed.

</details>


### [226] [Ground-Side Mission Plan Compilation with Policy-as-Code Guardrails for Cloud-Native Satellite Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14798)
*Hsiu-Chi Tsai,Chia-Tung Chung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Satellite Mission Compiler，一个四阶段流水线，将人工编写的卫星任务计划编译为容器工作流工件，供云原生卫星运行时（如ORCHIDE、AxDCU-1、Kepler）消费。流水线包含Pydantic模式解析、OPA/Rego策略评估、类型化的WorkflowIntent中间表示生成，以及Argo Workflow DAG/Kueue Job清单渲染。验证涵盖golden翻译评估、argo lint、进程内OPA决策复现及单节点集群提交（含Kueue v0.17.3/v0.18.3的DRA GPU准入级联和统一GPU+CPU设备类配额）。开源协议为EUPL-1.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云原生卫星运行时（ORCHIDE等）仅执行来自地面的工作流工件，缺乏将人工编写的任务计划编译为这些运行时所需工件的开源地面端工具链。ORCHIDE明确将“延迟阶段”之外的部分排除在其范围外，且未发布开源地面工具链。

Method: 四阶段流水线：(1) 基于源自ORCHIDE公开材料的Pydantic模式解析任务计划；(2) 用含10条deny规则的OPA/Rego策略包（有文档化出处）进行评估；(3) 编译为类型化的WorkflowIntent中间表示；(4) 渲染为Argo Workflow DAG和Kueue Job清单（支持DRA）。安全性设计基于NASA-STD-8739.8B的防御纵深（安全视角）而非NIST SP 800-53（安全视角/信息安全视角）。

Result: 通过了golden翻译评估、argo lint验证、进程内OPA决策复现，以及单节点集群提交验证：包括Kueue v0.17.3上的DRA GPU准入级联（v0.18.3复验）和v0.18.3上的统一GPU+CPU设备类配额（含调度级加速器回退）。发布6个MCP工具供AI代理使用。

Conclusion: 填补了云原生卫星运行时缺乏开源地面端编译工具链的空白，通过分层验证设计（防御纵深的安全视角）在编译阶段捕获上行前损失事件（分四个严重等级），降低了将错误任务计划部署到卫星的风险。

Abstract: Onboard cloud-native runtimes for satellites are emerging on multiple tracks (ORCHIDE, Axiom Space's AxDCU-1, Kepler's Jetson nodes), but each assumes that the workflow artifacts it executes arrive from the ground. ORCHIDE's architecture document D3.1 states explicitly that "only the Deferred Phase is part of the ORCHIDE scope," and no open-source ground-side toolchain has been released by the consortium. We present Satellite Mission Compiler, a four-stage pipeline that addresses this gap: it takes a human-authored mission plan, checks it against machine-checkable structural and policy rules, and compiles it into the container-workflow artifacts that cloud-native satellite runtimes consume. The pipeline parses the plan against a Pydantic schema derived from public ORCHIDE materials, evaluates it against an OPA/Rego policy package of ten deny rules with documented provenance, compiles it into a typed WorkflowIntent intermediate representation, and renders it as Argo Workflow DAGs and Kueue Job manifests with Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA) support. We classify pre-uplink loss events into four severity tiers tied to specific schema and policy checks, and anchor the layered-validation design in the safety reading of defense-in-depth (NASA-STD-8739.8B) rather than the security reading (NIST SP 800-53). The implementation is validated by golden translation evaluations, argo lint, an in-process baseline that reproduces OPA's decisions, and live single-node cluster submission, including a DRA-backed GPU admission cascade on Kueue v0.17.3 (re-validated on v0.18.3) and, on v0.18.3, a unified GPU+CPU device-class quota with a scheduler-level accelerator fallback. Six Model Context Protocol (MCP) tools expose the pipeline to AI agents. The compiler is released under EUPL-1.2 (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21228150).

</details>


### [227] [Don't Predict, Prioritize: Rethinking GPU Reliability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15115)
*Difeng Ma,Changhua Pei,Yuanwei Lu,Quan Zhou,Zexin Wang,Yibo Zhu,Daxin Jiang,Dan Pei,Jingjing Li,Gaogang Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HeaRank框架，将GPU故障预测从绝对时间预测转向相对风险排序，在数千GPU的生产集群上AUC达0.83，TOP 5%节点捕获64%故障，显著优于现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU故障的精确时间预测因强随机性和低信噪比而本质困难，需要范式转换。

Method: 基于Learning-to-Rank框架，利用稳定历史故障模式对GPU节点进行全局风险排序。

Result: AUC 0.83；线上部署中TOP 5%节点捕获64%故障，生产系统仅21%。

Conclusion: 相对风险排序可作为绝对故障预测的鲁棒替代方案，支持风险感知调度与主动资源管理。

Abstract: The reliability of Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) is a criticalbottleneck for modern large-scale AI infrastructure, where a sin-gle node failure can disrupt synchronous training jobs and causesignificant financial losses. While predictive maintenance is widelyused in other hardware domains, we demonstrate that accuratelypredicting the exact timing of GPU failures is inherently difficult.Through an in-depth analysis of telemetry data from a productioncluster, we find that major GPU failures, including Double Bit Er-rors (DBEs) and GPU Lost events, exhibit strong stochasticity andlow signal-to-noise ratios in time-series telemetry, which makesconventional time-based prediction ineffective.
  This insight motivates a paradigm shift: instead of attempting topredict the absolute timing of a failure, we propose a more robustapproach focused on ranking nodes by their relative failure risk. Wepropose HeaRank (Health Rank), a Learning-to-Rank (LTR) frame-work that leverages stable historical failure patterns to computea global risk ranking of GPU nodes. Evaluated on a production-scale cluster with thousands of GPUs, HeaRank achieves an AUCof 0.83, significantly outperforming both heuristic baselines andstate-of-the-art ranking algorithms. In online deployment, HeaRanksuccessfully captures 64% of future failures within the top 5% ofranked nodes, compared to only 21% by the incumbent productionsystem. These results suggest that relative risk ranking can serveas a robust alternative in environments where absolute failure pre-diction is inherently limited. Our work highlights the importanceof risk-aware scheduling and proactive resource management inmodern GPU clusters.

</details>


### [228] [Memory-Exhaustion Attack on the Blocklace Byzantine-Repelling Conflict-Free Replicated Data Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15185)
*Erick Lavoie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出blocklace协议虽然理论上能限制拜占庭节点产生的任意更新被正确节点复制为有限数量，但该数量可被恶意节点放大至标识符空间大小，从而淹没正确节点；实际部署需额外限制接受的标识符集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析blocklace协议在限制拜占庭节点任意更新复制方面的安全性漏洞，揭示其在面对恶意节点时可被利用的缺陷，并指出实际部署需要额外限制。

Method: 对blocklace的复制行为进行理论分析，证明其有限复制数量的上界可被恶意节点通过标识符空间放大，从而绕过原有安全保证。

Result: blocklace协议中正确节点最终复制的任意更新数量虽然是有限的，但该有限值可被放大至标识符空间的大小，足以淹没正确节点。

Conclusion: blocklace协议在实际部署中需要对接受的标识符集合施加额外限制，否则恶意节点可利用标识符空间发起DoS攻击。

Abstract: The blocklace is a directed acyclic graph encoding the causal relationship between authenticated updates produced by participating nodes. Compared to previous approaches, it adds restrictions on what can be replicated: a new update and its causal history is replicated locally if and only if either 1) it reveals a new node behaving arbitrarily (byzantine), or 2) it was signed by a node that still appears to be correct and the new updates provide evidence incriminating at least the set of nodes locally known to have behaved arbitrarily. The restrictions purport to limit the replication of arbitrary updates, even in the presence of colluders that never produce incriminating evidence, so that only a finite number will eventually be replicated by correct nodes.
  While the original description of the replication behaviour successfully achieve this aim, we show that this finite number can be made arbitrarily large, up to the size of the identifier space used to authenticate messages. This effectively enables malicious nodes to overwhelm correct nodes with arbitrary and useless updates. Practical deployments therefore require additional restrictions on the set of identifiers that will be accepted by correct nodes.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Campaign Diagrams: Visualizing the March Through the Phases of a Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15225)
*Toluwanimi O. Odemuyiwa,John D. Owens,Michael Pellauer,Joel S. Emer*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 提出"campaign diagrams"可视化技术，用于现代工作负载的资源利用率和瓶颈的阶段级分析。该技术在同一图中展示计算吞吐量、内存带宽利用率、流量和延迟，弥补了roofline（丢失时间信息）和profiler/跟踪（细粒度但掩盖瓶颈）之间的鸿沟。通过低秩GEMM和Mamba两个案例研究，展示了该技术能揭示难以通过roofline或profiler单独发现的优化机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具存在权衡：roofline将工作负载聚合为单一点，丢失时间维度信息；而profiler和跟踪虽暴露细粒度事件，却掩盖了性能瓶颈所在。缺乏一种能在阶段级同时展示资源利用、带宽、流量和延迟的可视化技术。

Method: 提出campaign diagrams可视化技术，在同一图中描绘计算吞吐量、内存带宽利用率、计算和内存流量以及延迟。该图可从分析模型、仿真或性能剖析数据生成，既能捕获理想边界，也能展示实际性能。

Result: 两个案例研究：1）低秩GEMM中揭示了反直觉结果——降低运算强度可提升端到端性能；2）Mamba中暴露了跨阶段的融合和流水线优化机会。

Conclusion: Campaign diagrams能揭示单独使用roofline或profiler难以识别的优化机会，是阶段级性能分析的有效可视化技术。

Abstract: We present campaign diagrams, a visualization technique for phase-level analysis of resource utilization and bottlenecks in modern workloads. Existing tools have a trade-off: rooflines aggregate a workload into a single point and lose all notion of time, while profilers and traces expose fine-grained events but obscure what bounds performance. Instead, a campaign diagram depicts compute throughput and memory bandwidth utilization, compute and memory traffic volume, and latency in a single figure. Since they can be generated from analytical models, simulations, or profiling data, campaign diagrams capture both ideal bounds and a kernel's achieved performance. We demonstrate them on two case studies: a low-rank GEMM, where they reveal the counterintuitive result that reducing operational intensity can improve end-to-end performance, and Mamba, where they expose fusion and pipelining opportunities across phases. In both cases, our visualization technique reveals optimization opportunities that are difficult to identify with rooflines or profilers alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [ExaGEMM: Exploration Framework for CPU-Driven ML Inference via Associative In-Register Computing for Low-Bit GEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14622)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Suyeon Jang,Hanning Chen,Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ExaGEMM是一个面向CPU的低比特GEMM协同设计与探索框架，通过寄存器驻留LUT执行和轻量级SIMD ISA扩展，在仅增加极少量硬件开销的情况下实现13.29倍延迟加速，并支持混合精度LLM工作负载的前沿选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特GEMM对高效ML推理越来越重要，但极低比特执行在传统CPU上表现不佳。实际部署涉及从1/2/4-bit权重的碎片化场景到不同激活精度，在固定SIMD和寄存器文件预算下，其可行性、重用机会和支持成本各不相同，使得轻量级CPU支持选择成为一个重要的设计问题。

Method: ExaGEMM通过寄存器驻留LUT执行实现低比特GEMM。核心洞察是现有SIMD数据通路已涵盖表生成和累加，唯一新增硬件是带有显式成本模型的寄存器内select/feed机制。该方法协同探索参数化内核和轻量级SIMD ISA支持，使用寄存器可行性、计算成本、内存流量和硬件开销的分析模型，在仿真前剪枝99.2%的候选空间，识别非支配支持点并生成ISA规范、gem5补丁和GEMM内核用于验证。

Result: 在代表性ML模型和CPU目标上，ExaGEMM相比纯软件基线实现13.29倍延迟加速，并表明对于混合精度LLM工作负载，工作负载感知的前沿选择尤为重要。

Conclusion: ExaGEMM验证了通过轻量级硬件-软件协同设计，可以在极低开销下显著加速CPU上的低比特GEMM，为混合精度LLM部署提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-bit GEMM is increasingly central to efficient ML inference, yet very-low-bit execution remains a poor fit for conventional CPUs. Practical deployment spans fragmented regimes-from 1/2/4-bit weights to varying activation precision-whose feasibility, reuse opportunity, and support cost differ under fixed SIMD and register-file budgets, making lightweight CPU support selection a first-class design problem. We present ExaGEMM, a workload-aware codesign and exploration framework for CPU-native low-bit GEMM via register-resident LUT execution. The key insight is that existing SIMD datapaths already cover table generation and accumulation; the only new hardware is an in-register select/feed mechanism with explicitly modeled cost. ExaGEMM co-explores parameterized kernels and lightweight SIMD ISA support using analytical models of register feasibility, compute cost, memory traffic, and hardware overhead, pruning the candidate space by 99.2% before simulation. It then identifies non-dominated support points and generates ISA specs, gem5 patches, and GEMM kernels for validation. Across representative ML models and CPU targets, ExaGEMM improves latency by 13.29x over software-only baselines, while showing that workload-aware frontier selection is especially important for mixed-precision LLM workloads.

</details>


### [231] [Toward Energy-Efficient and Low-Power Arrhythmia Detection for Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14747)
*Floriaan Bulten,Yawar Rasheed,Arlene John,Vincenzo Stoico,Ghayoor Gillani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究利用近似计算技术（数据精度降低和近似乘法器）降低深度学习模型在可穿戴心律失常检测设备上的功耗，在MIT-BIH心律失常数据库上实现了93.7%分类准确率，同时功耗降低64.9%（12kHz下3.07μW），显著延长了可穿戴设备电池寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可穿戴心电监测设备体积大、不舒适且依赖人工判读，而深度学习算法虽性能优异但计算复杂度和功耗过高，限制了其在可穿戴设备上的部署。因此，需要在不显著牺牲分类性能的前提下降低深度学习架构的功耗。

Method: 采用两种近似计算技术：数据精度降低（减少位宽）和近似乘法器（替代精确乘法运算）。在MIT-BIH心律失常数据库上训练和验证深度学习模型，并综合评估采用不同近似乘法器的硬件架构。

Result: 在12kHz下，提出的架构功耗为3.07μW（能量2.17μJ），相比基准架构8.75μW（2.08μJ）功耗降低64.9%，分类准确率93.7%，敏感性92.1%。在100MHz下，功耗9.45mW（能量0.8μJ），相比基准架构能耗降低61.5%。

Conclusion: 所提出的近似计算方案能在保持可接受心律失常分类性能的前提下，显著降低深度学习模型的功耗和能耗，从而有效延长可穿戴设备的电池续航时间。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases are the leading cause of death worldwide, and conditions such as arrhythmia often require long-term monitoring for effective detection and diagnosis. However, current wearable monitoring devices are bulky, uncomfortable, and typically rely on clinicians to manually evaluate electrocardiograms (ECGs). While Deep Learning (DL) algorithms have shown superior performance in arrhythmia detection and classification, their computational complexity coupled with high power consumption limit deployment in wearable devices. To address this challenge, this paper investigates the use of approximation techniques to reduce the power and energy consumption of DL architectures while maintaining acceptable classification performance. Specifically, techniques such as data precision reduction and approximate multiplication are investigated in a state-of-the-art DL model and its corresponding hardware architecture. The model is trained and validated using the MIT-BIH Arrhythmia Database, and hardware implementations employing various approximate multipliers are synthesized and evaluated. Compared with the state-of-the-art 8.75 μW (and 2.08 μJ) reference architecture, our proposed architecture consumes 3.07 μW (and 2.17 μJ) at 12 kHz, showing 64.9% reduction in power consumption while providing an acceptable output quality, i.e., 93.7% classification accuracy and 92.1% sensitivity. At 100 MHz, our proposed architecture consumes 9.45 mW (and 0.8 μJ), showing 61.5% reduction in energy consumption as compared to the state-of-the-art architecture. These results demonstrate that our proposed approximations significantly extend wearable device battery life while preserving the required arrhythmia classification performance.

</details>


### [232] [Valinor: Architectural Support for Fast, Energy-Efficient and Programmable Physical Memory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14789)
*Konstantinos Kanellopoulos,Spiros Galanopoulos,Konstantinos Sgouras,Vlad-Petru Nitu,Ilias Papalamprou,Andreas Kosmas Kakolyris,Rahul Bera,Dimosthenis Masouros,Dimitrios Soudris,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Valinor 提出了一种硬件-操作系统协同的内存分配方案，通过可编程硬件分配引擎执行操作系统提供的分配库，在不牺牲可编程性的前提下实现接近固定硬件性能的内存分配，加速比达17倍，端到端性能提升16%，能耗降低最高8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前系统中，每次缺页异常都会陷入内核，导致流水线冲刷、停顿以及长序列分配步骤，开销可达数万周期。对于无服务器函数和微服务等短生命周期工作负载，缺页异常可占用高达54%的运行时间和40%的系统能耗。现有硬件分配方案要么牺牲有用的放置优化，要么依赖无法适应新策略或硬件条件变化的固定功能逻辑。

Method: Valinor 引入了一个可编程硬件分配引擎，执行操作系统提供的紧凑分配库，速度接近固定功能硬件。它支持多种分配策略，包括短生命周期对象分配器、完整性机制以及硬件遥测指导的放置。研究在 BOOM RISC-V 软核上运行 Linux 和全系统模拟器上实现。

Result: 在真实硬件上，Valinor 实现了17倍的分配加速，16%的端到端性能提升，以及最高8%的能耗降低。全系统模拟进一步验证了可编程分配引擎和六个分配库，表明 Valinor 在不牺牲可编程性的前提下提供了硬件级别的性能。

Conclusion: Valinor 通过硬件-操作系统协同设计，在内存分配中实现了软件灵活性与硬件级性能的结合，有效解决了现代短生命周期工作负载中缺页异常开销过大的问题。

Abstract: Physical memory allocation establishes virtual-to-physical mappings on demand. In current systems, each minor page fault traps into the kernel and triggers pipeline flushes, stalls, and a long sequence of allocation steps that can cost tens of thousands of cycles. These overheads are increasingly significant for short-lived workloads such as serverless functions and microservices, where minor faults can account for up to 54% of runtime and up to 40% of system energy. Prior hardware allocation proposals avoid traps and context switches, but either sacrifice useful placement optimizations or rely on fixed-function logic that cannot adapt to new policies or changing hardware conditions.
  We present Valinor, a hardware-OS cooperative memory allocation substrate that combines software flexibility with hardware-class performance. Valinor introduces a programmable hardware allocation engine that executes compact OS-supplied allocation libraries at close to fixed-hardware speed. It supports diverse policies, including short-lived object allocators, integrity mechanisms, and hardware-telemetry-guided placement. We implement Valinor on a BOOM RISC-V soft core running Linux and in a full-system simulator. On real hardware, Valinor accelerates allocation by 17x, improves end-to-end performance by 16%, and reduces energy consumption by up to 8%. Full-system simulation further evaluates the programmable allocation engine and six allocation libraries, showing that Valinor provides hardware-class performance without sacrificing programmability.

</details>


### [233] [CODA: Algorithm-Hardware Co-design for Edge Video Diffusion via NMP-Enabled Compute-Cache Operator Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14908)
*Yuanpeng Zhang,YuXuan Wu,Yitong Xiao,Chenhao Xue,Yi Ren,Cong Li,Yihan Yin,Dimin Niu,Guangyu Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CODA是一种算法-硬件协同设计架构，通过将计算与缓存操作分离，在边缘GPU上实现视频扩散模型的高效部署，相比最先进的缓存算法获得最高1.80倍加速和1.74倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备上部署视频扩散模型(VDM)时，迭代式Transformer去噪过程过慢；跨时间步缓存(CTC)虽能减少冗余计算，但在内存受限的边缘GPU上缓存占用过大，且缓存操作与计算操作紧密交织，导致PCIe通信和序列化瓶颈。

Method: 提出CODA架构，核心思想是计算-缓存算子解耦(Compute-Cache Operator Disaggregation)：将密集计算路径与内存受限的缓存路径分别部署在xPU和轻量级DIMM端近内存引擎上；将碎片化的缓存活动重组为硬件友好的合并段；利用无分类器引导(CFG)分支独立性，实现xPU计算与缓存端执行的重叠。

Result: 相比最先进的缓存算法，CODA实现了最高1.80倍端到端加速、1.74倍能效提升，同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过算法-硬件协同设计的算子解耦策略，可以有效解决边缘设备上视频扩散模型部署中的缓存瓶颈问题，实现更高效、更节能的本地推理。

Abstract: Deploying Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) on edge devices is appealing for localized and privacy-preserving generation, but their iterative Transformer-based denoising remains too slow for practical local inference. Cross-Timestep Caching (CTC) has emerged as a promising direction for reducing redundant computation, reusing activations across adjacent denoising steps rather than modifying model weights, while largely preserving generation fidelity. However, on memory-constrained edge GPUs, CTC requires a massive cache footprint that quickly exceeds on-device VRAM and forces the cache into host memory. More fundamentally, cache operators remain tightly interleaved and chain-dependent with native compute operators, so naive near-memory offloading still incurs repeated PCIe exchanges for residual and fusion computations, turning cache reuse into a communication- and serialization-bound execution flow. We therefore propose CODA, an algorithm-hardware co-designed architecture centered on Compute-Cache Operator Disaggregation. CODA separates dense compute paths and memory-bound cache paths across the xPU and a lightweight DIMM-side near-memory engine, reorganizes fragmented cache activity into hardware-friendly coalesced segments, and exploits Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) branch independence to overlap xPU compute with cache-side execution. Experiments show that CODA achieves up to 1.80x end-to-end speedup and 1.74x higher energy efficiency, while preserving competitive generation quality compared with a state-of-the-art caching algorithm.

</details>


### [234] [Differentiable Routability-Driven Package Floorplanning with Pin Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15005)
*Yiqi Huang,Zepeng Li,Zhen Zhuang,Kehao Chen,Genggeng Liu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对先进封装中扇出布线可布线性问题，提出了一种可微分的布局规划与引脚分配算法，包括差异化线长最小化、交叉感知引脚分配和可微分可布线性最大化方法，在全部基准测试上实现100%可布线性，线长最多降低约23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进封装技术发展，重分布层（RDL）互连密度增加，可布线性对封装布局规划至关重要。同时，电源完整性要求保留扇入区域用于电源传输网络（PDN），迫使信号网走扇出区域，使得可布线性估计复杂化。现有均匀网格拥塞模型无法准确表征扇出拥塞，而先前引脚分配方法难以评估网交叉。

Method: 1) 差异化线长最小化：直接建模离散芯片方向，反向传播线长梯度至芯片位置和方向，避免连续角度建模的偏差；2) 交叉感知引脚分配：将网交叉代价融入多策略DPSO算法，采用GPU并行代价评估降低线长；3) 可微分可布线性最大化：构建针对扇出布线的拥塞模型，建立从拥塞信息到芯片位置的反向传播路径，引导可布线性优化。

Result: 在所有基准测试上实现100%可布线性；在基线方法成功布线的案例中，相比配备本文引脚分配流程的领先布局规划方法，线长最多降低约23%。

Conclusion: 所提算法有效解决了先进封装中扇出布线的可布线性问题，同时显著降低了线长。

Abstract: As advanced packaging technology evolves, increasing interconnect density in redistribution layers (RDLs) makes routability critical to package floorplanning. Meanwhile, power integrity requirements often reserve fan-in regions for the power delivery network (PDN), forcing signal nets through fan-out regions and complicating routability estimation. Existing uniform grid-based congestion models cannot accurately characterize fan-out congestion, while previous pin assignment methods struggle to evaluate net crossings. We propose a differentiable routability-driven floorplanning and pin assignment algorithm for advanced packaging with fan-out routing. First, a differentiable wirelength minimization method directly models discrete chip orientations and back-propagates wirelength gradients to chip locations and orientations. It reduces wirelength under fixed pin selection while avoiding the bias of continuous-angle modeling. Second, a crossing-aware pin assignment method incorporates net-crossing cost into a multi-strategy DPSO algorithm and uses GPU-parallel cost evaluation to reduce wirelength efficiently. Finally, a differentiable routability maximization method constructs a congestion model tailored to fan-out routing and establishes a back-propagation path from congestion information to chip locations, thereby guiding routability optimization. Experimental results show that our method achieves 100% routability on all benchmarks. For cases successfully routed by the baselines, it reduces wirelength by up to approximately 23% compared with a leading floorplanning method equipped with our pin assignment flow.

</details>


### [235] [Pattern-Guided Design Space Exploration for FPGA Accelerator Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15068)
*Jialiang Zhang,Weiman Yan,Yuelin Zou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PATTERNDSE 是一个轻量级的模式引导设计空间探索框架，通过识别 FPGA 内核中的重复计算模式（如逐元素映射、规约、矩阵运算等）来压缩调度空间，在保持 HLS 综合质量的前提下，将评估候选数从 140 减少到 29（4.83 倍搜索缩减），并恢复与穷举基线相同的 Vitis HLS 延迟结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLS 虽提高了 FPGA 加速器的设计抽象层次，但高质量的调度决策（如流水线、展开、分块、重排序、缓冲等）仍然依赖人工经验，且设计空间呈组合爆炸式增长。同时，许多数值内核呈现出重复的计算模式，这些模式可以指导不同的优化策略，但现有方法未能充分利用这一信息来有效缩减搜索空间。

Method: 将重复计算模式（逐元素映射、规约、矩阵-向量、矩阵-矩阵、模板更新等）映射到紧凑的调度空间；通过 LLVM 执行验证功能正确性；检查 HLS C 代码生成；使用简单的模式感知估计器对候选方案排序，最后提交 Vitis HLS 综合。

Result: 在 vecadd、axpy、dot、matvec、gemm、jacobi2d 六个代表性内核上，模式引导的 DSE 将 HLS 评估候选数从 140 降至 29，总体搜索缩减 4.83 倍，单个内核最高缩减 12.0 倍，且在所有内核上恢复了与穷举基线相同的最佳有效 Vitis HLS 延迟。

Conclusion: 计算模式信息可以有效剪枝非生产性的调度组合，同时保持高质量的 HLS 综合结果。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) raises the abstraction level of FPGA accelerator design from hardware description languages to C/C++, but high-quality results still depend on schedule decisions such as pipelining, unrolling, tiling, reordering, and buffering. These decisions create a combinatorial design space, while many numerical kernels exhibit recurring computation patterns that suggest different optimization strategies. This paper presents PATTERNDSE, a lightweight pattern-guided design space exploration (DSE) framework for FPGA kernels written in Allo, a scheduling-oriented HLS programming system. PATTERNDSE maps recurring computation patterns, including elementwise maps, reductions, matrix-vector operations, matrix-matrix operations, and stencil-like updates, to compact schedule spaces. It then applies candidate schedules, validates functional correctness through LLVM execution, checks HLS C code generation, and uses a simple pattern-aware estimator to rank candidates before Vitis HLS synthesis. We evaluate PATTERNDSE on six representative kernels: vecadd, axpy, dot, matvec, gemm, and jacobi2d. Compared with an exhaustive-lite baseline, pattern-guided DSE reduces the number of HLS-evaluated candidates from 140 to 29, achieving a 4.83x overall search reduction and up to 12.0x reduction for individual kernels. Across all evaluated kernels, PATTERNDSE recovers the same best valid Vitis HLS latency as the exhaustive-lite baseline, demonstrating that computation-pattern information can prune unproductive schedule combinations while preserving high-quality HLS outcomes.

</details>


### [236] [NIFA: Nonlinear IMC enhanced FPGA for efficient ML inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15123)
*Jiajun Hu,Ruthwik Reddy Sunketa,Lei Zhao,Archit Gajjar,Luca Buonanno,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无ADC的FPGA架构，用模拟内容可寻址存储器（ACAM）替代传统模数转换器，并在ReRAM存内计算模块内原生支持非线性运算和动态矩阵乘法，从而显著提升Transformer和CNN推理效率（能效最高提升40倍，面积效率最高4.1倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReRAM存内计算（IMC）模块仅支持静态权重向量矩阵乘法，非线性运算和动态矩阵乘法需交由FPGA逻辑完成，导致Transformer等依赖频繁非线性与动态运算的模型获益有限；且IMC模块中ADC占用超过70%的面积与功耗，严重制约系统效率与可扩展性。

Method: 1）提出无ADC的IMC模块架构，用ACAM替代传统ADC，在模拟域原生支持非线性运算；2）进行FPGA感知的设计空间探索，在FPGA面积、灵活性和深度学习性能间权衡，确定最优交叉阵列维度；3）开发利用ACAM高效映射动态矩阵乘法（DIMM）的计算映射方案，将IMC适用范围扩展至注意力计算。

Result: 在CNN和Transformer基准测试上，相比传统方案，能效分别提升高达40倍和1.9倍，面积效率分别提升4.1倍和2.5倍，且对长序列输入的Transformer工作负载保持稳健增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的ADC-free FPGA架构显著提升了FPGA深度学习推理效率，尤其突破了Transformer类模型在传统IMC-FPGA上的性能瓶颈，推进了领域专用FPGA设计的发展。

Abstract: Recent FPGAs have improved deep learning (DL) inference efficiency through dedicated tensor blocks and in-BRAM computation. ReRAM-based analog in-memory computing (IMC) pushes efficiency further, offering an order-of-magnitude improvement in compute density and energy efficiency over conventional digital logic by performing vector-matrix multiplication (VMM) directly within the ReRAM crossbar; prior work has integrated such IMC blocks into FPGAs for DL inference. However, conventional IMC designs support only static-weight VMM, leaving nonlinear operations and dynamic matrix-matrix multiplication (DIMM) to the FPGA fabric. As a result, the benefits of IMC are largely confined to static-weight models, whereas Transformer-based models, which rely on frequent nonlinear and DIMM operations, gain only limited improvement. Moreover, the ADCs within each IMC block consume more than 70% of its area and power, further limiting system efficiency and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose a novel FPGA architecture that integrates an ADC-free IMC block, replacing the conventional ADC with analog content-addressable memories (ACAMs) that natively perform nonlinear operations inside the block. To fully exploit this block, we conduct an FPGA-aware design-space exploration that determines optimal crossbar dimensions while balancing FPGA area, flexibility, and DL performance, and we develop an efficient mapping that leverages ACAMs to carry out DIMM operations, extending the applicability of IMC to attention computation. On CNN and Transformer-based benchmarks, the proposed architecture achieves up to 40x and 1.9x higher energy efficiency and 4.1x and 2.5x higher area efficiency, respectively. Overall, it significantly improves FPGA DL inference efficiency and sustains robust gains on Transformer-based workloads across long input sequences, advancing domain-specialized FPGA design.

</details>
